<div class="feature-row">
<div class="feature-row__item feature-row__text feature-row__text--right">
<h2 class="h3">WHAT ARE SKINS?</h2>
<div class="rte rte-setting featured-row__subtext"><p>Skins are thin textured sheets that wrap around your devices to completely change the look and feel of the device without adding any thickness, unlike conventional cases and covers. It also protects your devices from scratches and fingerprint stains. </p></div>
</div>

<script>
  jQuery(function() {
    jQuery('.accordion-container :radio').change(function() {
      
      var optionIndex = jQuery(this).closest('.accordion-container').attr('data-option-index');
      var val = [];
      
	  // Picks up changes in the individual radio buttons.   
      
      val = val.join('+');
      if(val === '	' || val === ''){
      	var optionValue = 'None';
        $("#AddToCart-product-customized-template").addClass('disable-button');
      }
      else{
        var optionValue = val;
        $("#AddToCart-product-customized-template").removeClass('disable-button');
      }
      jQuery(this)
        .closest('form')
        .find('.single-option-selector')
        .eq(optionIndex)
        .val(optionValue)
        .trigger('change');
    });

    replaceImgDataSrc();
    lazyLoadHowToPage();
    lazyLoadProductDesc();
    //lazyLoadProductSlider();
    loadAnnoucementBar();
  });
  jQuery(function() {
    jQuery('.swatch :radio').change(function() {
      var optionIndex = jQuery(this).closest('.swatch').attr('data-option-index');
      var optionValue = jQuery(this).val();
      jQuery(this)
        .closest('form')
        .find('.single-option-selector')
        .eq(optionIndex)
        .val(optionValue)
        .trigger('change');
    });

    replaceImgDataSrc();
    lazyLoadHowToPage();
    lazyLoadProductDesc();
    //lazyLoadProductSlider();
    loadAnnoucementBar();
  });
  //Check if cookie is set display the announcement bar appropriately
  function loadAnnoucementBar(){
    cookieBool = false;
  	isCookiePresent = readCookie(cookieBool);
    if(!isCookiePresent){
       $('#top-announcement-bar').show();
       $('#top-announcement-bar').css('position', 'relative');
      
    }
  }
  
  //Reading the cookie if not set and return the boolean
  function readCookie(cookieBool){
  	var cookieArr = document.cookie.split(";");
    if(document.cookie.indexOf('hide=' + 'true') !== -1){
        cookieBool = true;
      }
    return cookieBool;
  }

  	
   //Add a click handler on the close button and set a cookie to store value
    $('.close-icon').on('click', function(){
      $('#top-announcement-bar').fadeOut();
       document.cookie = "hide=true";
    })
 
  
  //Dynamically replacing all img src attributes to img data-src to trigger lazy loading
  function replaceImgDataSrc(){
  	$(".description-wrapper img").each(function() {
      var imgSrc = $(this).attr("src");
      $(this).attr("data-src", imgSrc);
      $(this).removeAttr("src");
    });
  }
  
  //LazyLoad Instance
  function lazyLoadInst(delay){
  	$('.lazy-load').lazy({
      effect: "fadeIn",
      effectTime: delay,
      threshold: 0
    }); 
  }
  
   //Lazy loading images for how to apply page
  function lazyLoadHowToPage(){
     $(".product-section .img-wrapper img").addClass("lazy-load");
//      $('.product-section .img-wrapper img').attr('src', 'https://cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/1684/4603/files/Brand_Icon.svg?10988');
    lazyLoadInst(200);
  }
  
   //Lazy Product Slider for how to apply page
  function lazyLoadProductSlider(){
  $("#jssor_1 img").each(function() {
      var imgSrc = $(this).attr("src");
      $(this).attr("data-src2", imgSrc);
      $(this).removeAttr("src");
    });
    
//    $("#jssor_1 img").addClass("lazy-load");
//       lazyLoadInst(100);
  }
  
  //Lazy loading images for Product Description
  function lazyLoadProductDesc(){
  	$(".description-wrapper img").addClass("lazy-load");
    lazyLoadInst(500);
  }
  
  //Accouncement bar sticky and fixed logic
  $(window).scroll(function(){
    if ($(window).scrollTop() > 0) {
      $('#top-announcement-bar').addClass('stick-scroll');
      $('#top-announcement-bar').removeClass('stick-top');
    }
    else {
      $('#top-announcement-bar').addClass('stick-top');
      $('#top-announcement-bar').removeClass('stick-scroll');
    }
  });
  
  //Search Bar functionality on how-to-apply page
  function onSearchDevice(){
	var input, filter,column, helpText, brandText, i, txtValue,cleanText,finalText,outerWrapper,headerText;
    var counter = 0;
    input = document.getElementById('searchInput');
    filter = input.value.toUpperCase();
    column = document.getElementsByClassName('col');
    headerText = document.getElementsByClassName('headerText');
	helpText = document.getElementsByClassName('helpText')[0];
    
    // Loop through all list items, and hide those who don't match the search query
    for (i = 0; i < column.length; i++) {
      brandText = column[i].textContent;
      cleanText = brandText.trim();
      cleanText = cleanText.split()[0];
      finalText = cleanText.replace(/\n/g, " ");
      if (finalText.toUpperCase().indexOf(filter) > -1) {
        column[i].style.display = "";
      } else {
        column[i].style.display = "none";
        counter++;
      }
    }
    
    //hide show header text
    if(input.value){
      for (j = 0; j < headerText.length; j++) {
        headerText[j].style.display = "none";
      }
    }
    else{
      for (j = 0; j < headerText.length; j++) {
        headerText[j].style.display = "";
      }
    }
  	
    //Toggle helptext
    if(counter === column.length){
    	helpText.style.display = "block";
    }
    else{
    	helpText.style.display = "none";
    }
  }//..onSearchDevice
  
  
</script>
